美時 (1795 TT)  Trading Buy

2026 年毛利率回升，海外 ECB 再融資計畫落地將降低利
息負擔。  
投資建議 ：(1) Alvogen 併購效益與全球 B2B 業務，產品組合優
化趨勢確立。營收規模翻倍且產品組合改善帶動毛利率回升；
(2) 高壁壘產品線進入密集收割期 ， 重磅 505(b)(2) Cabozantinib
預計 4Q26 審查完成。 (3) 預估 2026 EPS 12.08 元，投資建議
Trading Buy ，目標價 200 元。  
2Q26 在併購營收規模擴張下，毛利率與獲利修復動能，但淨利
仍受到併購融資之高額財務利息與一次性稅負壓抑：  
2Q26 營收 88.09 億，毛利率為 53.68% ，較 1Q26 的 50.73% 提
升，主要反映高毛利產品出貨回升與產品組合轉佳。費用顯著
攀升主要係合併 Alvogen 後營運規模擴張所致。  
營業外收支部分， 1H26 非營業費用 25.76 億，其中財務成本達
23.67 億 (YoY+980.82%) ，主要受到美國較高利率環境下產生龐
大財務利息成本，以及期間美元升值產生之外幣匯兌損失衝擊。
所得稅方面， 1H26 認列非經常性一次性稅務項目，若排除該項
一次性影響，調整後有效稅率為 22.3% 。  
2H26 美時將以 ECB 再融資顯著減輕利息成本為獲利修復核心 ：  
受惠於併購 Alvogen 後的完整年度營收挹注 、 7 月起併入 Sandoz
菲律賓業務、韓國 Uro-Vaxom 經銷啟動，以及 Nintedanib 與
Enzalutamide 等 B2B 產品持續在全球多國鋪貨出貨， 2026 全年
營收將維持翻倍增長曲線。隨著 2Q26 產品組合優化使毛利率
回升至 53.7% ，預期 2H26 在高毛利癌症藥物與自製困難學名藥
出貨比例提升下，全年毛利率將維持在 52%–54% 區間。  
美時海外可轉換公司債 (ECB) 發行案已正式獲得證期局核准，將
積極推進債務再融資計畫，目標是大幅置換現有高利率貸款、
顯著降低下半年及未來的財務利息費用，進一步釋放獲利彈性
並提升資金調度靈活度。

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 2 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
美時之企業使命「提供全球病患更好、更安全且更負擔得起的藥物」。為追
求使命，同時投入環境與社會之永續發展， 2024 年初，美時已成立「永續
暨風險管理小組」 ，針對公司的永續發展工作建立管理機制，研擬永續發展
相關之短、中、長期目標、策略，並管理監督跨部門之執行過程與成果，以
從系統性的內部管理，極小化美時產品對於社會及環境所帶來的潛在衝擊。
公司簡介：  
美時專精於高門檻的複雜學名藥及癌症用藥的研發、生產與銷售。公司產品
線涵蓋中樞神經系統、癌症、心血管疾病等領域，並在美國、歐洲及亞洲多
國市場擁有廣泛的銷售通路。  
透過策略性併購與全球市場的布局，美時已逐步從區域性藥廠轉型為具備
全球影響力的國際製藥公司。  
2Q26 在併購營收規模擴張下，毛利率與獲利修復動能，但淨利仍受到併購
融資之高額財務利息與一次性稅負壓抑：  
2Q26 營收 88.09 億， 1H26 累計營收 174.53 億 (YoY+84.18%) ，創下歷史半
年度新高紀錄。 2Q26 毛利率為 53.68% ，較 1Q26 的 50.73% 提升 3 個百分
點，主要反映高毛利產品出貨回升與產品組合轉佳。 1H26 營業費用合計
48.22 億 (YoY+93.65%) ，其中管銷費用為 42.96 億 (YoY+97.06%) 、研發費用
為 5.26 億 (YoY+69.68%) ，費用顯著攀升主要係合併 Alvogen 後營運規模擴
張所致。  
營業外收支部分 ，1H26 非營業費用合計高達 25.76 億 ， 其中財務成本達23.67
億 (YoY+980.82%) ，其他營業外淨支出為 2.09 億 (YoY-51.40%) ，主要受到美
國較高利率環境下產生龐大財務利息成本，以及期間美元升值產生之外幣
匯兌損失衝擊。所得稅方面， 1H26 認列非經常性一次性稅務項目，使本期
實際有效稅率暫時提升至 29.5% ；若排除該項一次性影響，調整後有效稅率
為 22.3% 。  
區域營收佔比與年增：  
市場區域 1H26 佔
比 1H26 金額變動(YoY) 變動說明 
美國 62% +68.97 億(+180%YoY) 併購 Alvogen 業務 
韓國 17% -0.27 億(微幅下滑) 既有成熟產品線自然波動 
B2B 業務 8% +10.42 億(+245%YoY) Nintedanib及 Enzalutamide全
球授權夥伴拉貨爆發 
台灣 7% -0.35 億(微幅下滑) 健保藥價定期調整 
東南亞 5% +0.78 億 區域通路擴展及產品送件核
准放量 
亞洲其他 1% +0.21 億 新興市場授權合作 
1H26 營收在地理分佈上呈現重大轉變，美國市場在 Alvogen 完整合併後營
收大幅增長 180% 達 68.97 億，營收佔比攀升至 62% ，成為公司最核心的營
運市場； B2B 全球外銷業務表現極為亮眼，受惠於 Nintedanib 於歐洲 27 國、
加拿大、台灣與美國上市出貨，以及 Enzalutamide 於加拿大、巴西、以色列

3 群益投顧 
與韓國上市放量， B2B 營收暴增 245% 達 10.42 億，佔比升至 8%；亞洲既有
市場整體保持平穩 (+1%YoY) ，其中東南亞增加 0.78 億、亞洲其他增加 0.21
億，有效抵銷韓國 (-0.27 億 )與台灣 (-0.35 億 )的微幅回檔。  
2H26 美時將以 ECB 再融資顯著減輕利息成本為獲利修復核心：  
受惠於併購 Alvogen 後的完整年度營收挹注、 7 月起併入 Sandoz 菲律賓業
務、韓國 Uro-Vaxom 經銷啟動，以及 Nintedanib 與 Enzalutamide 等 B2B 產
品持續在全球多國鋪貨出貨， 2026 全年營收將維持翻倍增長曲線。在毛利
率表現上，隨著 2Q26 產品組合優化使毛利率回升至 53.7% ，預期 2H26 在
高毛利癌症藥物與自製困難學名藥出貨比例提升下，全年毛利率將維持在
52%–54% 區間。  
美時海外可轉換公司債 (ECB) 發行案已正式獲得證期局核准，將積極推進債
務再融資計畫，目標是大幅置換現有高利率貸款、顯著降低下半年及未來的
財務利息費用，進一步釋放獲利彈性並提升資金調度靈活度。  
中長期來看，管線中超過三分之一的專案將於 2026 至 2028 年密集推出，產
品上市能見度極高，將推動公司在 2026 年之後維持成長動能。  
投資建議 ：(1) Alvogen 併購效益與全球 B2B 業務，產品組合優化趨勢確立。
營收規模翻倍且產品組合改善帶動毛利率回升； (2) 高壁壘產品線進入密集
收割期，重磅 505(b)(2) Cabozantinib 預計 4Q26 審查完成。 (3) 預估 2026 
EPS 12.08 元，投資建議 Trading Buy ，目標價 200 元。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Partnership expanded across AI infrastructure, local AI computing and n
automotive: Alongside the pricing, Nvidia and MediaTek announced a deepening of
their longstanding collaboration spanning three areas, including 1) AI
infrastructure: MediaTek will adopt Nvidia’s NVLink Fusion platform to enable
customers to develop custom AI infrastructure designed to integrate with Nvidia

rack-scale systems and AI factories. 2) Local AI computing: the companies will
continue to collaborate on multiple generations of RTX Spark™ and DGX Spark™ PC
chips integrating Nvidia GPUs with MediaTek SoCs. 3) Automotive: MediaTek and
Nvidia will continue developing platforms for AI-powered, software-defined vehicles
in the era of physical AI.
Key highlights from the joint interview
Following the announcement, the CEOs of Nvidia and MediaTek were interviewed jointly

by Bloomberg TV. Below we summarize key highlights from the interview.
Nvidia characterized the partnership with MediaTek as a multi-generation and n
long-term collaboration. Nvidia commented that the two companies are working
together across multiple generations of edge AI and PC SoCs, while the expanded
partnership will also cover data-center XPUs, networking and robotic systems.
Nvidia added that it is a decade-long roadmap.
The expanded collaboration will allow MediaTek’s XPUs to connect directly into n

Nvidia’s broader AI factory architecture. Nvidia noted that it will connect its
NVLink Fusion scale-up system, Spectrum-X switches and broader networking
infrastructure to MediaTek’s XPUs.  According to Nvidia, this would allow MediaTek
XPUs to be integrated into data centers already running Nvidia infrastructure, while
MediaTek XPU project wins could also create opportunities for Nvidia networking
products.
MediaTek expects the partnership to support growth in its AI ASIC business, n

although the new revenue contribution will take time. When asked whether the
collaboration could accelerate MediaTek’s AI chip business, MediaTek said that it
expects this to happen, but noted that it would take some time as the company is
only beginning to integrate Nvidia’s latest NVLink version into the relevant chassis.
MediaTek highlighted faster time-to-market for customers as a key benefit.
Both companies indicated that customer engagement around NVLink Fusion has n

already started. Nvidia said the collaboration was in large part driven by customer
requests and that there are already customers committed to NVLink Fusion.
Nvidia positioned NVLink Fusion as a way to accommodate custom XPUs within n
its existing infrastructure rather than as a replacement for its broader platform.
Nvidia noted that XPUs are already present in the market and said it is opening its
platform to allow customers’ specialized XPUs to connect more easily to Nvidia

infrastructure. Nvidia added that it expects to continue participating through
networking, switching and CPUs, even in AI factories where some racks incorporate
XPUs.

Goldman Sachs MediaTek (2454.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM

### Page 3

Nvidia indicated that the partnership enables MediaTek to offer a more n
complete end-to-end XPU solution. Nvidia noted that an XPU provider would
otherwise need to address multiple system-level components, including NVLink
switching, NICs, Ethernet switching and scale-up/scale-out networking. Through the
partnership, these components can be provided through Nvidia’s existing
infrastructure.
MediaTek plans to use the bond proceeds across supply-chain and technology n

investments. MediaTek said supply-chain investment will be one use of proceeds
and emphasized that the company’s supply chain is global. The company also plans
to invest further in design capabilities, ASIC chip capabilities, IP and interconnects,
and advanced packaging, with the proceeds supporting the expanded Nvidia
collaboration.
The collaboration also extends to Nvidia’s custom HBM architecture. Nvidia n
indicated that its next-generation custom HBM will use an Nvidia-designed base

layer that has been extended to NVLink Fusion partners, including MediaTek and
Amazon. Nvidia noted that the NVHBM and XPU die will be integrated into a CoWoS
package, allowing partners to leverage Nvidia’s installed base and supply-chain
scale.
MediaTek identified the supply chain as its primary bottleneck. MediaTek said n
supply-chain challenges extend beyond HBM to substrates and other components,
while noting that its location in Taiwan and close collaboration with Nvidia are

helpful. Nvidia added that its networking ecosystem is becoming part of MediaTek’s
supply chain, while MediaTek’s XPU is becoming part of Nvidia’s supply chain.
Nvidia sees a solid standalone business case in its MediaTek investment. In n
response to questions on financing, Nvidia noted that MediaTek operates its own
independent business and is already a highly profitable and successful company.
Nvidia indicated the investment is grounded in the companies’ deep, multi-year

technology partnership, with the current roadmap extending over a decade, and is
intended to demonstrate Nvidia’s confidence in and commitment to the
collaboration. Nvidia also expects the investment to generate attractive returns.
Our thoughts - Strong ecosystem endorsement, broader system-level
opportunity and potential new TAM into custom HBM base die
Strong endorsement from two leading AI ecosystem partners
We believe the most immediate takeaway is the strong endorsement from both Nvidia

and Alphabet. Nvidia subscribed to US$3.5bn of MediaTek’s US$3.9bn CB issuance,
while Alphabet and global institutional investors participated in the remaining
US$400mn. In our view, Nvidia’s capital commitment provides strong validation of
MediaTek’s silicon, connectivity and advanced packaging capabilities. Alphabet’s
participation also reinforces MediaTek’s relevance across multiple major AI ecosystems.
Funding should strengthen supply-chain security and support continued technology

investment. According to the company, one of the original purposes of the financing
was to secure capacity and supply for constrained components such as HBM.
Although certain customers currently procure HBM and other components directly, we
1 September 2026   3
Goldman Sachs MediaTek (2454.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

believe MediaTek could assume greater supply-chain responsibility under future
engagement models, including entering into capacity or supply agreements on behalf of
customer projects.
The company also indicated that it plans to invest further in its technology and design
capabilities, including ASIC design, IP , interconnects and advanced packaging, etc. We
believe the additional funding should provide MediaTek with greater flexibility to
secure the resources required for increasingly complex AI related programs.

The partnership broadens MediaTek’s role from custom AI silicon toward system-level
enablement
Based on the interview and the company announcement, we believe MediaTek is seeking
to extend its role beyond designing and delivering an individual AI ASIC toward helping
customers address a broader portion of the system surrounding the XPU. This could
include connectivity, memory architecture, advanced packaging, manufacturing and
integration with rack-scale AI infrastructure.

However, we do not believe this means MediaTek intends to build or sell complete
AI racks. Rather, MediaTek can help customers integrate their custom XPUs into
Nvidia’s pre-validated chassis, networking and rack-scale architectures, allowing
customers to deploy and scale their AI infrastructure more efficiently.
NVLink Fusion is intended to provide customers with a faster path from custom XPU to
AI factory. Customers can focus their resources on the differentiated compute portion of

the accelerator while relying on Nvidia and MediaTek for surrounding technologies such
as NVLink connectivity, high-speed SerDes, memory architecture, packaging,
manufacturing and system qualification.
Instead of independently engineering and qualifying every component surrounding the
custom XPU, customers can use NVIDIA ’s prebuilt, prequalified and system-prevalidated
platform as a starting point. We believe this should reduce integration complexity and

risk, shorten time to market and make it easier for customers to deploy custom XPUs
within existing Nvidia-based data centers and scale them into rack-level AI factories.
Incremental revenue will likely take time, but the partnership expands MediaTek’s
longer-term opportunity
MediaTek and Nvidia did not provide a timeline for the first NVLink Fusion-related
customer, product or revenue contribution. The company indicated that it is only
beginning to integrate the latest version of NVLink, after which customers would still

need to complete their own product development, qualification and deployment
processes. We therefore see limited immediate earnings implications and believe the
incremental opportunity is more likely to emerge over the medium to long term.
Nevertheless, the partnership broadens MediaTek’s addressable opportunity from
designing an individual AI ASIC toward enabling a production-ready system. We
believe this could improve customer win rates and increase MediaTek’s potential content

and value capture per project through additional contributions from connectivity,
memory architecture, advanced packaging, manufacturing and system integration.

### Page 5

The relationship extends across the full edge-to-cloud AI computing stack 
Beyond AI infrastructure, the partnership covers multiple generations of DGX Spark and 
RTX Spark chips for local AI computing, as well as automotive platforms. We therefore 
view the announcement as a broader, multi-generation technology alignment spanning 
cloud AI factories, enterprise and local AI computing, PCs and physical AI, rather than a 
single-product collaboration. 
Management noted that the Nvidia partnership should not restrict MediaTek’s existing 
custom ASIC engagements. We see NVLink Fusion as an additional architecture option 
rather than an exclusive platform. Customers will be able to decide whether to adopt the 
Nvidia ecosystem depending on their infrastructure and workload requirements, while 
MediaTek can continue supporting customers using other architectures. 
In our view, Alphabet’s participation in the CB issuance should allay any concerns 
some investors may have had that the closer Nvidia relationship could affect 
MediaTek’s existing hyperscaler engagements. Instead, we believe MediaTek’s 
positioning is to remain architecture-flexible while providing customers with a broader 
range of design and system-integration options. 
NVHBM collaboration extends MediaTek’s participation into the memory and 
packaging architecture 
We note that Nvidia’s next-generation NVHBM will use a custom HBM base layer 
designed by Nvidia, which has also been extended to NVLink Fusion partners including 
MediaTek and Amazon. Nvidia noted that NVHBM will be integrated with the XPU die in 
a CoWoS package, allowing partners to leverage NVIDIA ’s broader HBM and packaging 
supply chain. While the NVHBM base layer itself is NVIDIA-designed, we believe 
MediaTek’s participation could deepen its involvement in memory architecture, 
interconnect and advanced packaging integration for XPU programs. Separately, 
MediaTek has highlighted custom HBM and memory-interface capabilities as part 
of its broader XPU offering, which we believe could provide incremental content 
opportunities for customer-specific designs outside Nvidia’s standardized NVHBM 
implementation. 
Investment Thesis, Price Target Risks and Methodology

Investment Thesis - MediaTek 
MediaTek is a leading global IC design house specializing in smartphone AP (application 
processor). We have a positive stance on MediaTek, viewing it as well-positioned to 
transition from a traditional smartphone application provider to an AI-focused vendor, 
beginning with AI smartphones and extending to enterprise ASICs and smart automotive 
solutions (in partnership with NVIDIA) in 2025 and beyond. We expect MediaTek to 
achieve solid multi-year growth, where we expect revenue and earnings to increase by 
43%/59% CAGRs, respectively, in 2025-28E. This growth will be primarily driven by: 1) 
market share gains, particularly in the premium segment (specifically high-end 5G 
flagship SoCs), 2) strong ramp in AI ASIC business, and 3) new TAM in the 
automotive/computing sectors.

competition that would result in reduced profitability, and (4) Slower ramp in ASIC would

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
MediaTek is a fabless IC design company supplying to 
wireless communications and digital consumer markets. 
Before taking leadership in mobile SoC area, it had built 
solid ground in the TV SoC and optical disk drive markets. 
The firm invests heavily in R&D to seek better positioning 
in the 5G cycle, and has a complete offering including ASIC, 
Wi-Fi, PMIC etc. to address applications beyond 
smartphone, such as IoT and auto.

Investment Rationale 
We have Buy rating on MediaTek as we think MediaTek is 
ready and has the strength to brace for the upcoming 
proliferation of IoT devices and opportunities in growing 
markets like ASIC, ARM-based PC, auto, and etc., and we 
believe its solid balance sheet and cashflow could provide 
downside support to the share price. Meanwhile, we expect 
5G smartphone penetration to be a multi-year theme for 
the company, with its better competitive landscape in 
duopoly 5G era and an improved margin profile.

Stock Data  
Price to Book Value 14.4x

### Page 3

Price objective basis & risk 
MediaTek (MDTKF) 
Our PO of NT$5,230 is based on 23x 2H27-1H28 P/E. as we are constructive on its 
broadening ASIC pipeline to drive earnings upgrade cycle and re-rating as it locks in 
more supply.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) better than expected smartphone demand, especially in 
China, (2) a better pricing in mobile communication segment thanks to alleviated 
competition and rising exposure to high-end/flagship market, and (3) an even more 
superior investment return on non-op side to uplift its net cash position. (4) Unexpected 
collaboration with global technology leaders leads to higher than expected 
margins/profits

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) weaker-than-expected end-market situation in 
consumer-related smartphone/TV space, (2) macro challenges including inflation/policy-
related pressures, (3) rising competition from existing players and/or newcomers in 
smartphone/TV SoC (system on chip) space, and (4) ASP/margin pressure caused by each 
or all of the above and/or rising foundry costs. (4) Bidding process for Meta Inference 
ASIC is slower than expected.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating.  VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation).

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We hosted an online conference call for NYPCB on 2 
September, after trading hours. Overall, we believe the price hike trend is 
largely on track with our estimates despite a short hiccup in 4Q26E (for 
more details, please refer to our earlier note, Escalating price hike in 
substrates). Looking ahead, we believe the trend towards enlarging body 
sizes and multiple core layers in ABF substrates and the supply-demand 
imbalance in BT substrates will be key growth drivers for NYPCB.

What’s the impact: Near-term update. We are comfortable with our 
revenue growth estimate of c.20% QoQ in 3Q26E, given ongoing price 
hikes and a rising utilisation rate at Kunshan (likely to reach 100% in 
4Q26E). We believe the yield rate at Kunshan still has room to improve, 
which could negatively impact the gross margin (GM) performance in 
3Q26E. That said, compared with July, we expect the GM to improve 6-7pp 
MoM in August given the rising yield rate, and remain confident of a c.30% 
GM in 3Q26E. We expect NYPCB to achieve higher price hikes than other 
substrate vendors, as it does not sign long-term agreements with end 
customers. However, NYPCB has stated that it currently has no intention to 
hike BT substrate prices further from 4Q26, as raw material price hikes 
have eased recently; as such, we have accounted for it in our model. 
Lastly, NYPCB mentioned that it has received new AI HDI orders from a 
US-based ODM, which will result in its PCB segment reaching c.30% QoQ 
revenue growth and operating profit from 4Q26E.

Long-term investment thesis. We believe a meaningful spec upgrade is 
also likely to occur in ABF substrates, as core layer counts will need to 
increase their rigidity to handle the increasing body size of substrates. This 
trend would digest more ABF capacity and relevant raw materials (eg, T-
glass) than just unit growth from 2028E. Also, we believe this would make 
the current upcycle last longer than previous ones. As for BT substrates, 
we believe ASP growth will accelerate again as two key players are 
gradually reducing their BT capacities. Thus, we maintain our BT ASP 
assumption for 2027E despite a lower-than-expected price hike in 4Q26E.

What we recommend: While we lower the 2026E EPS by 4% on a 
reduced BT price hike in 4Q26E, we keep the 2027-28E EPS largely 
unchanged. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and raise our 12-month TP to 
TWD2,460 (from TWD2,444), based on an unchanged target PER of 49x 
applied to the 1-year forward EPS. Downside risk: weaker-than-expected 
demand for switches.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 10-41% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more bullish view on ABF/BT ASP hikes.

2 September 2026

Company profile 
Nan Ya PCB originated as one of the business divisions of Nan Ya Plastics Corporation before 
transforming into a subsidiary in 1997, specialising in the manufacturing of printed circuit boards (PCBs) 
and integrated circuit (IC) substrates. The company’s primary end applications include networking 
(c.50%), PCs (c.20%), consumer electronics (c.10%), automotive (c.10%) and other sectors. Its 
manufacturing facilities are located in Taiwan and China.

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Nan Ya PCB's board of directors (BoD) is responsible for overseeing the company's operations
and ensuring adherence to corporate governance principles. The board comprises individuals
with diverse expertise, contributing to effective decision-making and strategic planning. The
company emphasizes the importance of corporate governance as a set of processes, customs,
policies, laws, and institutions affecting the way the corporation is directed, administered, or
controlled. Principal stakeholders include stockholders, management, and the board of directors,
among others.
Capital management 2
Nan Ya PCB maintains a prudent approach to capital management, ensuring financial stability
and the availability of resources necessary for operational and strategic initiatives. The
company's financial statements reflect a solid capital structure, supporting its growth and
sustainability objectives.
Related party &
transaction 1
The company adheres to strict policies regarding related party transactions to maintain
transparency and fairness. Such transactions are conducted under terms comparable to those
with independent third parties and are disclosed in financial reports to ensure compliance with
regulatory standards and corporate governance best practices. For example, the company's
consolidated financial statements provide details on transactions with related parties, ensuring
transparency and accountability.
S Supply chain
management 1
Nan Ya PCB is committed to sustainable supply chain management, emphasizing environmental
protection, procurement policies, and social responsibility. The company has established a
comprehensive procurement policy that integrates sustainability considerations, ensuring that
suppliers adhere to ethical standards and environmental regulations. This commitment is part of
the company's broader sustainable responsibility policy, which includes areas such as corporate
governance, environmental protection, and social welfare.
E Data security 1
While specific details on Nan Ya PCB's data security measures are not extensively disclosed in
the available sources, the company recognizes the importance of information security in its
operations. As part of its corporate governance framework, Nan Ya PCB is likely to implement
standard data protection practices to safeguard sensitive information and ensure compliance
with relevant regulations. Stakeholders are encouraged to consult the company's official
communications or contact the company directly for detailed information on data security
policies.

Airtac
Valuation
We derive our target price of NT$1,750 for Airtac by using a P/E methodology, as we believe the margin and earnings growth
outlooks will be the key drivers of the stock. We apply a P/E of 27.5x for 2H26-1H27E, which we derive from the mid range of
prior upcycle valuation 25-35x during 2021 in view of a strong upcycle with solid FA demand and ongoing share gains.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the Airtac stock from reaching our target price include: 1) a softer-than-expected China

macro economy, resulting in downside to our sales forecasts; 2) intensifying competition leading to gross margin pressures;
and 3) slower-than-expected progress of its linear guide business, causing low utilization of capacity.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

•Broad-base 2Q, cost-capped margin. Mgmt. reiterated that 2Q26 growth 
was broad-based across every product line, end market and region, not a 
single AI pocket. GPM was slightly below Street, as raw material cost 
inflation offset part of the pricing and mix improvement. Internal 
manufacturing efficiency projects are underway to absorb it in the future.
•Jul price hike hits spot and direct. The Jul price adjustment covers spot 
prices and direct customers, vs. earlier rounds limited to contract 
customers, and spans c.50% of revenue including MLCC, tantalum and 
other capacitors. Spot pricing flows through immediately rather than at 
renewal, so mgmt. expects ASP and revenue effects shortly, with margin 
impact far more visible in 2H. Mgmt. also sees no sign of demand slowing 
down in the near-term.
•Tantalum now the largest line. AI is not an MLCC-only story for passive 
components. Tantalum grew fastest of any line in 2Q, suits AI power 
management, and has over taken magnetics to become Yageo's largest 
product line. MLCC also grew well and should keep growing in coming 
quarters. AI revenue is now 16% of total revenue, spread across tantalums, 
MLCCs, resistors and inductors rather than single product.
•65 sites, dual-region by product. Every product is manufactured in at least 
two regions, giving customers flexibility against tariff and geopolitical risks. 
Mgmt. believe the existing 65 facilities are flexible in adding new lines at a 
fast pace whenever shortages appear, saving customers 1-2 years vs. 
building from greenfield. The company also planned the capacity expansion 
ahead of demand.

### Page 2

Yageo (2327.TW)
04 September 2026 Citi Research
2
•YFIII and overseas MLCC bases. YFIII in Kaohsiung, commissioned in 2024, is Yageo's largest MLCC fab and still has
space for additional MLCC lines, with AI-specific equipment arriving monthly since last year. Outside Taiwan, Yageo
has been opening new lines within existing facilities in Mexico and China, leaving it able to absorb incremental AI
orders without a new building cycle.
•Lead times extend, LTA interest rises. Lead time of most of the products has lengthened vs. 1H26. Tantalum is the

tightest, followed by MLCC and resistors. Mgmt. indicated all AI-related lines are extremely tight, and more
customers are now approaching Yageo to sign LTAs.
•NP0 leadership on the power side. Yageo estimates a meaningful share (at least 30-40%) of NP0 high-voltage
MLCC, based on the internal assessment. Built on EV and industrial experience, NP0 fits AI's high-voltage, high-
temp duty and sits on the DC power side, while high-cap MLCC serves the near-chip stage. Yageo ships direct to

leading power supply players and is invited as first batch of design partners.
•Low-to-high conversion only partial. Converting low-end MLCC capacity to high-end is only partially possible.
High-spec MLCC stores more capacitance through higher layer counts, requiring advanced stackers rather than
conventional equipment. Yageo can optimize and redeploy some existing tooling, but not fully substitute, which is
why it is committing capex to new AI production line instead of relying on conversion.

Yageo
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,450, based on 35x average 2027E-28E earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the
company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market. Our target multiple of 35x is
at the higher end of the trading range since 2022. During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and
pricing competition; 3) execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and interest rate fluctuations.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

ab 6 September 2026
Global Research
Largan Precision
Strong CPO ramp priced ahead of qualification
The re-rating has outpaced qualification progress; downgrade from Buy to Sell
Largan shares have risen 242% since April on optimism around its CPO FAU progress.
While we stay constructive on Largan's CPO prospects, we believe the market is pricing
in confidence ahead of still-early qualification progress, particularly given a crowded
competitive field. Largan only starts its sample line late Q3, integrating its Prism Micro

Lens Array and Fiber Array with AOET's V-groove and Corning's fiber. We expect  several
quarters to improve yields, gain integrator acceptance (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and
convert its initial production-oriented FA program into volume while competing against
incumbent TFC and vertically integrated rivals incl. Coherent. We downgrade our rating
from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-
18x range well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 PT based on 15x smartphone lens

+ 50x CPO base case for 40% share  (upper end of optical peers  at 14-72x).
Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low) and
1 FA design in an FAU. A risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028

and also competes with incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and
other FAU vendors like Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not
requiring Largan's initial V-groove approach. Largan's push toward a complete solution
may also conflict with integration partners.
Core smartphone lens tracking behind amid memory headwinds
We also see risk on near-term results with Q326 sales tracking light. Aug sales were NT
$5bn, +3% MoM, -16% YoY, taking QTD revenue to NT$10.1bn, 53% of UBSe/Street

Q326. Sept would need to grow +73% MoM to meet UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ vs.
normal seasonal +8% MoM. We expect flattish builds at best for its flagship customer
(80% of sales) needing to raise price to pass on memory costs +US$200-300 YoY albeit
some support from high-end introducing variable-aperture lens, while foldable lens
specs remain similar. Factoring muted smartphone growth but healthy margins, we view
a mid-teens multiple appropriate for this business, in line with its historical range.

Stock prices in our upside case ahead of traction; risk-reward skews negative
We maintain basic '26E/27E EPS at NT$190/NT$214 and NT$5,000 SOTP PT based on 1)
15x smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730
(40% share, 100k switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of
peers due to its premium ~40-45% OpM. We view risk/reward skewed negative with
downside to NT$2,500 on limited CPO success and softer smartphones, base case NT

$5,000 for CPO traction, and upside 22% if the stock is bid to our upside case NT$9,000
based on top-end 70x P/E on CPO earnings at 60% share of a higher US$1.58bn TAM.

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 2
Largan Precision UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Could Largan's CPO products contribute meaningful revenue in a few years?
We stay constructive but the company still has hurdles to clear in a crowded space. Its scale and track
record in high-end smartphone lens gives it an opportunity supplying key components of the CPO

FAU assembly, traditional strength in precision lens micro assemblies, alignment accuracy, in-house
tooling and automation that could give it a differentiated solution. Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan
suggests the company is still early in qualification, with its sample production line for customer
evaluation readied in late September and still requiring work with a system integrator and
qualification with the fabless customer and ODMs.  We still see a year to move from sampling to

design-in but if successful timed to meet expected market acceleration in 2028-29.
Q: Will there be further technology upgrades driving the lens cycle in the coming years?
We expect spec upgrades for Largan's core lens business to come in H226 from variable aperture
shipments for the iPhone 18 (which Genius does not currently participate though may enter over
time, as seen with periscope lenses). Key offset though is weaker sales with Q3 tracking light and

suggesting a softer build due to memory headwinds. Further upside could come from broader
adoption of 48MP modules in future iPhone models. Looking longer term, Apple is reportedly
exploring 200MP periscope lens and adjacent opportunities to penetrate physical AI categories with
precision lens including robotics, autonomous systems and AR/VR devices.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell as we view the market expectations as now
pricing in a bullish scenario for strong market traction with the company still at an early stage of

qualification, still needing to improve its process and yields, and competing in a crowded space also
against an existing incumbent. We expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain
acceptance from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI GPU
fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and other rivals. We downgrade
Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x

range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens +
50x CPO base case for40% share  (upper-end of optical peer range of 14-72x).
Largan has demonstrated its samples with partners at Computex and Semicon Taiwan and cited its
goals to ramp CPO as its next opportunity. The company is still early in qualifications with a sample
automated line in the coming months followed by design-in engagement with system integrators,

ODMs and the fabless customer to target design-in on 3rd gen CPO solutions in 2028. For
smartphone lens, its top customer had a strong iPhone 17 cycle although acknowledged it would
have to raise price to pass on higher memory costs which could dampen growth. Sales QTD are
tracking slightly light as Largan also competes with 2nd source Genius. Our supply chain checks and
management commentary points to introduction of higher value variable aperture lens in H226.

Largan is currently trading above the high end of its historical range at 39x/35x our 2026E/27E EPS vs.
a long-term range of 13-18x forward consensus earning, pricing in success penetrating the CPO
component market ahead of the earnings stream which would only start late in 2027 into 2028. We
note optical component peers trade at 14-72x with an average of ~38x 2027E P/E (Genius at 25x
2027 P/E). We maintain our SOTP valuation with our price target at NT$5000 based on 1) 15x

smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730 (40% share, 100k
switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of peers due to its premium ~40-
45% OpM. We view the stock at NT$7,400 now pricing in CPO traction on a 50x multiple of our
upside NT$76 case based on 60% share of an upside US$1.6bn opportunity.

### Page 3

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 3
Largan Precision UBS Research
commentary
Largan shares have risen 242% since 1 April on optimism around its CPO fiber array unit
(FAU) progress. While we stay constructive post our feedback at Semicon Taiwan on
Largan's ability to develop CPO solutions, we now believe the market is pricing in a level
of confidence that has run well ahead of qualification progress which is still early and
competing in a crowded field. Largan will only start its sample line in late Q3 integrating

its Prism Micro Lens Array and Fiber Array, AOET's V-groove and Corning's glass fiber.
From there, we expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain acceptance
from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI
GPU fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and rivals including
Coherent with own lens/FAU. The company did note on its Q226 call that it had secured
one mass production commitment for its fiber array to an FAU maker that could ramp by

H227 although early to conclude the magnitude of this engagement or end customer
design-in, with the other customers still at proof-of-concept phase.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and
the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT
$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens + 50x CPO base case for 40% share
(upper-end of optical component peers trading at a  range of 14-72x).

Source: UBS estimates
Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low). A
risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028 and also competes with

incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and other FAU vendors like
Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not requiring Largan's initial V-
groove approach. Largan's business model may also conflict with integration partners as
it also attempts to build toward a complete solution.
We estimate Largan's Total Addressable Market in a downside case is US$270mn if it
only supplies sub-components into a market that continues to push out CPO due to

reliability concerns relative to pluggables and NPO/XPO options, US$1.08bn in a base
case with 100k switches and 72 FAUs per switch at US$150 ASP , and US$1.6bn in an
upside case with 125k switches, 72 FAUs per switch and US$175 ASP .  For Largan, we
estimate downside case 10% share yields NT$810mn sales and NT$2.20 EPS, base case
40% share, NT$13.0bn sales and NT$35 EPS, and upside case 60% share, NT$28.4bn
sales and NT$76 EPS.
Although we are optimistic on the market opportunity scaling up and Largan's

competencies and progress developing these solutions, Largan's traction is not yet
certain and it will compete in a crowded landscape. We believe Himax is already
supplying wafer level optics for the collimator function with FOCI (3363.TWO) and TFC
(300394.SZ) already has demonstrated capability and design-in on the FAU and lens
assembly. Others that could vertically integrate include Coherent, Senko, Corning and
Sumitomo, and we also note Genius sending samples and Sunny Optical's intention to

develop for this market.

Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan indicates Coherent can make the FAU and lens by itself
through capabilities merging with II-VI and demonstrated all in-house components at
the booth except for the metal transceiver casing.

Smartphone lens business tracking behind, should have a mild 2026-27
Largan reported August sales at NT$5bn,   is below seasonal 63-67% at only 53% of
Q326 UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ and down 16% YoY, suggesting muted momentum
into its flagship customer (~80% of sales). July EPS was NT$13.94, 27% of UBSe Q3 NT
$51.52 suggesting in-line GM/OpM at ~50%/39% but the lighter sales are more of a

concern.
UBS expects flattish builds at best and a mild spec upgrade with one model variable
aperture but foldable at similar lens spec and higher memory cost (+US$200-300 YoY).
We still see mild growth and mid-teens multiple acceptable for the smartphone
business, similar to its long-term average.

Largn (3008.TW) trading at 7,400 at closed Sept 4.

UPSIDE (NT$9,000): Our upside valuation of NT$3,655 is based on  its average P/E of 16x
off our 2027 upside case EPS of NT$235. We factor in faster than expected
CPO progress to deliver upside EPS of NT$76 and 70x P/E at the high end of optical peers
trading at 14-72x 2027E P/E to an implied valuation of NT$5,345, and  total share price at
NT$9,000.
BASE (NT$5,000):  Our price target for its smartphone business is NT$3,270, with a 15x
2027 P/E applied on its base case EPS of NT$214 slightly below the long-term average of

its historical P/E band to reflect the macro uncertainty. Paired with our base scenario of its
CPO business at NT$35 EPS and 50x P/E, we arrive at an implied valuation of NT$1,730,
and Largan's aggregate PT  at NT$5,000.
DOWNSIDE (NT$2,500):   Downside valuation at NT$2,392, based on low-end of its
range 13x 2027 P/E. We take into consideration slower adoption of FAUs at its downside
EPS of NT$2 and the same 50x P/E to  an implied valuation of NT$108 for

CPO contribution, and Largan's total share price at NT$2,500.

Forecast returns
Forecast price appreciation -32.4%
Forecast dividend yield 1.1%
Forecast stock return -31.4%
Market return assumption 6.3%
Forecast excess return -37.6%
Company Description
Founded in 1987, Largan Precision (Largan) is one of the world's leading manufacturers of
lenses used in cameras and IT products. It specialises in plastic/hybrid lenses and, to a lesser
extent, glass lenses and modules. In 2022, 20MP+ camera lenses were 10-20% of Largan's

shipment mix, 10MP lenses were 50-60%, 8MP lenses were 0-10% and other lenses were
30-40%.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on SOTP valuation on the CPO opportunity and traditional lens
business.
We think Largan's downside risks include: 1) ongoing pricing pressure from smartphone OEM
clients and increasingly intense competition among lens suppliers; and 2) a slower-than-
expected smartphone demand recovery in China, and 3) CPO commercial success below the

market's elevated expectations.
We believe the key upside risk is a faster-than-expected CPO qualification process at Largan.
In addition, if the company demonstrates exceptionally efficient and highly accurate
automation capabilities, it could support greater market share gains within the industry than
we currently anticipate.

Quantitative Research Review
UBS Global Research publishes a quantitative assessment of its analysts' responses to certain questions about the likelihood of an occurrence
of a number of short term factors in a product known as the 'Quantitative Research Review'.  The views for this month can be found below.
Views contained in this assessment on a particular stock reflect only the views on those short term factors which are a different timeframe to

the 12-month timeframe reflected in any equity rating set out in this note.  For previous responses please make reference to (i) previous UBS
Global Research reports; and (ii) where no applicable research report was published that month, the Quantitative Research Review which can
be found at https://neo.ubs.com/quantitative, or contact your UBS sales representative for access to the report or the Quantitative Research
Team on ubs-quant-answers@ubs.com. A consolidated report which contains all responses is also available and again you should contact your

UBS sales representative for details and pricing or the Quantitative Research Team on the email above.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
August  Sales  +4% MoM/+156%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Chroma's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for share price movement.
Details:
• August revenue was NT$5,632mn (+4% MoM/+156% YoY).
• QTD revenue reached 75% of MSe, at NT$14,772mn (+9% QoQ/+130% YoY),
and 79% of consensus, at NT$13,992mn (+3% QoQ/+118% YoY).
Our view:
• We see 3Q26 tracking ahead of market expectations and likely to deliver

over 20% QoQ.
• Looking ahead, we expect its power testing business to stay at an elevated
level, and the semiconductor & photonics business to continue growing HoH
into 2H26, driven by SLT, metrology, CPO and potential shipment of FT
handlers/burn-in ovens.
• Stay OW.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
Base case, 45x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it better
captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average valuation of
~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating profit CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG of 1.0. We think
our target multiple of 45x is justified by the 61% operating profit CAGR we project for

2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected macro conditions
n Stronger semi and photonics and power testing demand
n Margin improvement via better product mix
n Better opex control
Risks to Downside
n Macro slowdown
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower progress in the advanced packaging capacity expansion
n Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand
n Margin contraction arising from lack of revenue scale and increasing opex

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Yageo
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,450, based on 35x average 2027E-28E earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the
company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market. Our target multiple of 35x is
at the higher end of the trading range since 2022. During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and
pricing competition; 3) execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and interest rate fluctuations.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Document 096: 260908_gs_semicon-TW

Source PDF: all report pdf/260908_gs_semicon-TW.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-08
Pages: 9

### Page 1

1. Photonics IC scaling up: AI drives data transmission speed and bandwidth 
requirement, leading to strong demand on Photonics IC, requesting it to catch up 
Electrical IC automated production and testing ecosystem in 1 to 2 years, while EIC / 
semiconductors has been developed for decades. The strong demand brings 
opportunities to tool suppliers, across coupling and testing, which are necessary 
tools to accelerate PIC production. Robotechnik / FiconTEC describes the numbers of 
tools they target to ship in coming 1 year would be over 50% of what they’ve shipped 
in past 25 years, reflecting the strong demand and orders visibility.  
2. Testing - CPO earliest beneficiary: As a new technology, CPO naturally takes time 
and faces challenges in the path of commercialization; however, we identify testing 
equipment suppliers as the earliest beneficiaries, considering the expensive final 
module means multiple testing during the production process is necessary, given the 
earlier a failure is found, the lower cost a failure is. As a result, companies we visited 
continue to see Insertion 2 (double sided wafer-level testing) as necessary, and 
Robotechnik / FiconTEC launched the new generation in 3Q26, which fully complied 
with the global leading foundry’s communication infrastructure. 
3. Testing - shortening time with KGD (know good die) at early stage: To facilitate 
photonics IC scaling up, not only are more tools needed but better tools, requiring 
suppliers’ strong R&D and execution, delivering continuous specification upgrade. 
Robotechnik / FiconTEC, as global leaders in PIC testing, aim to shorten the 
wafer-level testing time by 50-60% next year, and target 4x faster die-level testing 
next year. Enlitech (7728.TWO, Not Covered) offers NightJar, a hyperspectral image 
system, revealing AWG (arrayed waveguide grating) optical loss pathways with 
absolute optical power mapping, allowing customers to KGD at Insertion 1 (PIC wafer

### Page 2

testing) or Insertion 0 (PIC wafer early defect screening), saving failure costs. 
4. Coupling - automated production becoming necessary in high-speed connection: 
AI drives optical module towards high speed and high density / bandwidth, driving 
demand on automated production. GMT (4573.TWO, Not Covered) showcased dual FA 
active alignment equipment for 1.6T optical module, which could do the coupling for 
both Tx and Rx simultaneously, saving 50% of coupling time compared to traditional 
equipment. FitTech (6706.TW, Not Covered) showcased optical engine assembly system, 
featuring with passive / active alignment and customized development of coupling 
algorithms, which could be used in multiple products: high-speed optical modules, ELS, 
OCS, NPO, CPO, SiPh and optical chiplets. ASMPT showcased MEGA multi-chip bonding 
solution for complex multi-chip and photonics packaging applications, combining 
high-precision placement with adhesive dispensing, UV fixation and 3D inspection in one 
platform. 
5. FAU & Laser specification upgrade: With optical networking expanding from scale 
out to scale up, FAU and laser specification is also migrating. We estimate FOCI‘s FAU in 
2026E to be mainly 40 channels (starting in 3Q26) for 3.2T, followed by 80 channels 
(within 2026E) for 6.4T, and 100 channels under R&D. The company’s FAU enjoys several 
customers across fabless, foundry, laser suppliers, etc. FOCI Aug revenues accelerated to 
+20% MoM (vs. +9% MoM in Jul), reflecting the ramp up of high-end FAU. VPEC‘s 
optoelectronics revenues enjoy strong growth on the back of strong CW lasers demand 
and InP substrate supply getting better. Its InP epiwafer for CW lasers is also migrating 
from 70mW / 100mW (scale out) to above 100mW (scale up, NPO) and 300mW / 
400mW (scale up, CPO), along with expansion from CW lasers to 100G / 200G EML, 
supporting its growth ahead. 
6. AI drives CMP slurry: Anji Micro owns a comprehensive customer base, allowing it to 
ride on growing AI spending across China and overseas. The global-tier AI infrastructure 
continues to migrate, driving the CMP slurry consumption per system, along with AI 
server racks ramp up, enlarging the total addressable market. As the CMP slurry local 
leader, we are positive on Anji’s capability to offer qualified and reliable semi material 
products to penetrate global-tier clients.   
Company highlights

Robotechnik (300757.SZ, Buy, 12m TP at Rmb796) Chairman & Head of FiconTest 
visit: We hosted Robotechnik Chairman and Head of FiconTest and visited the booth in 
Semicon Taiwan on Sep 3. Management of Robotechnik highlights the strong demand on 
Photonics IC automated production and testing, driving their tools demand. The 
company’s strong beat in 2Q26 results (report link) reaffirms our positive view on 
Robotechnik, riding on the growing trend across CPO, NPO, OCS, and optical modules. 
We identify Robotechnik to be the major beneficiary in the photonics IC scaling trend, 
considering its comprehensive tools coverage and strong automation capability across 
software and hardware. Read more: Robotechnik / FiconTEC Semicon Taiwan 
takeaways.  
VPEC (2455.TW, Buy, 12m TP at NT$793), Chairman visit: We hosted VPEC Chairman 
and CFO in Semicon Taiwan on Sep 3. Management remains positive on their

optoelectronics business, riding on the AI infrastructure cycle, specification upgrades,
VPEC’s customers’ penetration, and product expansion. We remain positive on VPEC and
the company’s strong Jul and Aug revenues reaffirm the strong AI data center optical

Taiwan takeaways.
Enlitech (7728.TWO, Not Covered), Chairman visit: We hosted Enlitech Chairman,
CEO, and CTO in Semicon Taiwan on Sep 3. Key discussions focused on CPO testing and

how the company’s product, NightJar, facilitating KGD (know good die) at early stage,
supporting customers to save failure costs. NightJar is a hyperspectral image system,
which could seamlessly upgrade the existing eVue microscope on PIC wafer-level testing
equipment (insertion 1), allowing customers to know the AWG (arrayed waveguide
grating) optical loss pathways with absolute optical power mapping. Read more:
Enlitech Semicon Taiwan takeaways.

FOCI (3363.TWO, Buy, 12m TP at NT$833), CFO visit: We hosted FOCI CFO in Semicon
Taiwan on Sep 3. Management remains positive on their CPO FAU business and expects
strong earnings growth ahead, riding on AI server racks ramp up and FAU specification
upgrade (from 40 channels to 100 channels). FOCI’s FAU started shipment in 3Q26,
driving Aug revenues growth accelerating to +20% MoM (vs. +9% MoM in July). We
expect CPO FAU revenue growth to further accelerate in 2H27, driving CPO FAU revenue

contribution up to 58% / 73% in 2027 / 28E (vs. 21% in 2026E), supporting GM
expansion to 37.9% by 2028E (vs. 18.8% in 2025A). Read more: FOCI Semicon Taiwan
takeaways.
GMT (4573.TWO, Not Covered): We visited GMT booth in Semicon Taiwan on Sep 3.
GMT showcased four launched system covering wafer-level optical inspection and
testing, as well as optical-engine active alignment for 1.6T optical modules. The
company highlighted automation and simultaneous TX/RX coupling, saving coupling

time by 50% compared to traditional methodology. Read more: GMT Semicon Taiwan
takeaways.
Fittech (6706.TW, Not Covered): We visited Fittech booth in Semicon Taiwan on Sep 3.
The company showcased equipment solutions for lens and FAU inspection,
high-precision optical coupling and bonding, PIC chip testing, etc. Management expects
FitTech to ride on the rising optical networking device end demand in AIDC and
specification upgrade towards 1.6T, 3.2T, CPO, NPO, driving the company’s growth

ahead. Read more: Fittech Semicon Taiwan takeaways.
Anji Micro (688019.SS, Buy, 12m TP at Rmb509): We visited Anji Micro booth in
Semicon Taiwan on Sep 3. The company demonstrated products incl. CMP Slurry, Post
CMP Cleaner etc. during the event. Key discussions focused on (1) CMP Slurry product
upgrade, (2) semi client demand, and (3) diversified customer base. Overall,
management is positive on the rising capex spending from both memory and advanced

logic clients, along with the specification upgrade towards high-end products. Read
more: Anji Micro Semicon Taiwan takeaways.
ASMPT (0522.HK, Neutral, 12m TP at HK$177): We visited ASMPT booth in Semicon
Taiwan on Sep 3. ASMPT showcased its advanced packaging solutions for AI, HPC and
silicon photonics and CPO applications, including TCB and HB technologies, alongside
the MEGA platform for high-precision multi-chip assembly. Read more: ASMPT Semicon

Taiwan takeaways.
Price target methodology and risks - ASMPT
Valuation methodology: We are Neutral-rated on ASMPT with a 12-month target price
of HK$177, which is based on 24.9x 2030E discounted P/E (discounted back to 2027E).
Our target P/E is derived from correlation between peers P/E to the sum of NI YoY and
OPM.
Key upside/downside risks: 1) customers’ faster-/slower-than-expected adoption of
Advanced Packaging tools; 2) stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand from

automotive customers; and 3) stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for traditional
IC packaging and SMT equipment.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Anji Micro
Valuation: We are Buy-rated on Anji Micro with a 12-month target price of Rmb509,
which is based on 46x 2030E discounted P/E (discounted back to 2027E). Our target P/E
is derived from correlation between peers P/E to the sum of NI YoY and OPM.
Key downside risks: 1) supply chain risks; 2) weaker semi client demand; and 3)

weaker-than-expected product expansion.
Valuation: Our 12-month target price of NT$833 is based on a 15.4x 2030E P/E,
discounted back to 2027E at a COE of 12.6%. Our target multiple is derived from peers’
correlation between forward-year trading P/E and earnings growth.
Key downside risks: Fiercer-than-expected market competition, technology migration
trends in optical networking, and slower-than-expected capacity ramp-up.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - VPEC

Valuation: We adopt a discounted P/E method and apply a 22.2x target P/E on VPEC’s
2029E EPS, discounting back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6%
and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12m TP of NT$793. Our target P/E is
based on a 0.4x PEG&M ratio derived from the average level of peers in the photonics
supply chain.
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh business ramp up; weaker-than-expected
microelectronics demand from smartphones clients; IDM offers EPI wafer in-house.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - RoboTechnik
Valuation: Our 12-month target price of Rmb796 is based on a 20.7x 2031E P/E,
discounted back to 2027E at a COE of 11.3%. Our target multiple is derived from the
correlation between consumer electronic peers’ PEG&M (2026E P/E to 2027E NI YoY and
OPM), with RoboTechnik’s 2032E net income at 2% YoY and 2032E OPM at 32%.
Key downside risks: Fiercer-than-expected market competition; lower-than-expected

optical connection end demand; worse-than-expected photovoltaic market downcycle.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

hiccup resulting from supply chain management.

GPU/ASIC server clusters and general servers is strong, driving  orders for its
chassis and rack offerings.

Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy ,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover . Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy , hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety , and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.
